Invoked by /paperloom:ingest. ([trapoom555/claude-paperloom](https://github.com/trapoom555/claude-paperloom/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-linker.md) · ★95)
- **finding-linker**: Compares new findings against a shortlist of existing wiki findings and proposes typed edges (supports / contradicts / extends / uses / similar-to). Invoked once per /paper-ingest. Returns JSON only; `lib apply-edges` writes and mirrors them. ([marvinsxtr/claude-librarian](https://github.com/marvinsxtr/claude-librarian/blob/HEAD/claude_librarian/agents/finding-linker.md) · ★3)
- **finding-merger**: Mechanical — groups review findings that describe the same defect, so each defect is verified and fixed once ([zb-ss/claude-plugin-workflow](https://github.com/zb-ss/claude-plugin-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-merger.md) · ★1)
- **finding-merger**: Owns Phase 5 — ingests every discovery artifact across all rounds, clusters findings by root cause rather than symptom, runs a falsification pass that re-reads the cited code to attempt disproof, and emits audit-output/05-findings-triaged.md. Use after Phase 4 discovery completes, or standalone to merge and falsify a pile of raw findings from multiple sources. ([calc1f4r/Horus](https://github.com/calc1f4r/Horus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/finding-merger.md) · 2 copies)
- **finding-prover**: Refute gate for one normalized review finding: tries to explain the finding away from the source and, failing that, proposes one cheap check that would settle it. Holds Read, Grep and Glob only, so it runs nothing. Dispatched by the proof stage of the review plugin's findings normalization (/review:fanout, /review:council); not intended for direct ad-hoc use. ([melodic-software/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/melodic-software/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/review/agents/finding-prover.md) · ★32)
- **finding-quality-auditor**: Audits finding quality and removes low-quality findings. ([vstakhovsky/shokunin-review](https://github.com/vstakhovsky/shokunin-review/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/finding-quality-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **finding-refuter**: Adversarially tries to refute a single code-review finding and returns SURVIVES, REFUTED, or PRE-EXISTING with the evidence behind each of the six refutation questions. The caller decides when to dispatch it; the review that invoked it runs the same pass itself by default. ([AlexJSully/AlexJSully-Portfolio](https://github.com/AlexJSully/AlexJSully-Portfolio/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/audit-pr/agents/finding-refuter.md) · ★3)
- **finding-refuter**: Phase 5 adversarial refuter, judges reviewer findings BEFORE the address-all loop spends a fix on them. Takes every finding of a review round verbatim and returns one verdict each, UPHELD (claim survives, cite the reachable entry point or the real rule), REFUTED (cite the file:line guard, carve-out, or missing catalog ID that makes it wrong, plus what would change your mind), or ESCALATED (real and worse than stated). Defaults to KEEPING the finding, uncertainty is never a refutation, because dropping a real defect costs more than fixing a phantom. Dispatch exactly ONE refuter per review roun… ([nadyshalaby/hackify](https://github.com/nadyshalaby/hackify/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-refuter.md) · ★3)
- **finding-refuter**: Skeptic who tries to REFUTE one code-review finding by constructing a concrete failing scenario from the actual code at a given sha. Returns real=true only with a reproduction path; defaults to real=false when uncertain. Used to filter findings before a developer is asked to fix them. ([shishir10/claude-sous](https://github.com/shishir10/claude-sous/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/finding-refuter.md) · ★3)
- **finding-refuter**: Adversarial reader whose job is to KILL a proposed QA finding before it is filed. Burden of proof reversed — it defaults to REFUTED when uncertain and must be forced to concede. Checks the six ways a finding is usually wrong here — harness artifact, working as designed, evidence does not show what it claims, wrong causal chain, unreachable state, already known. Use from /ct-qa (one agent per candidate finding, in parallel) before anything reaches FINDINGS.md. Returns a verdict; never edits and never files. Advisory tier — non-blocking. ([CT2689-Tech/DeclutrMail](https://github.com/CT2689-Tech/DeclutrMail/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/finding-refuter.md))
- **finding-refuter**: Adversarially verifies ONE review finding by trying to REFUTE it against the actual code — reads the implicated files, dependency sources, and configs, and returns a verdict with line-level evidence. Defaults to not-real when the failure mechanism cannot be confirmed. Used by adversarial-diff-review as the gate between "plausible finding" and "fix it". ([Drozdetskiy/claude-skills](https://github.com/Drozdetskiy/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-refuter.md))
- **finding-reporter**: Phase 14 per-finding report authoring agent. Reads a single finding directory (draft.md, debate.md, adversarial-review.md, poc script, evidence/) and writes the disclosure-ready report.md via the vuln-report skill. Runs cold-context per finding so the heavyweight PoC-building workload cannot starve the report-writing step. ([vigolium/piolium](https://github.com/vigolium/piolium/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-reporter.md) · ★152)
- **finding-responder**: Author-side reception of code-review findings for `/review-respond`. Re-verifies each finding against current codebase reality (not the reviewer's word) and classifies it agree / disagree (reasoned) / needs-human — never performative agreement. Under --fix only, applies agreed findings one at a time via the sanctioned lib/respond.mjs apply path. Advisory by default. ([Tahmid12Khan/agentic-workflows](https://github.com/Tahmid12Khan/agentic-workflows/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-responder.md))
- **finding-reviewer**: Independent reviewer for one candidate finding of a dmrgpy hole hunt, briefed to refute it. Spawn one per candidate finding. Use only as part of the hole-hunt skill's process. ([joselado/dmrgpy](https://github.com/joselado/dmrgpy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/finding-reviewer.md) · ★115)
- **finding-reviewer**: Reviews changes to checks, the engine, or the AI layer for review-safety and contract violations. Use after adding or changing a check, before committing. ([deepak-terse/agentic-coding](https://github.com/deepak-terse/agentic-coding/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/finding-reviewer.md))
- **finding-reviewer**: Independently reviews findings reported by Logbook's review agents (bug-hunter, security-scanner, import-tester and the rest) and decides, for each one, whether it is a real issue to record in docs/phases/known-issues.md, a duplicate, not a bug, a choice that needs an open question, or something the reporting agent must investigate further — in which case it writes the exact questions to send back. - **finding-reviewer**: Independently reviews findings reported by Renovo's review agents (bug-catcher, security-scanner, performance-auditor and the rest) and decides, for each one, whether it is a real issue to record in docs/phases/known-issues.md, a duplicate, not a bug, a choice that needs a decision first, or something the reporting agent must investigate further — in which case it writes the exact questions to send back. Used by the issue-sweep skill; also use it directly when asked "is this a real bug", "check this finding" or "should this go in known issues". Re-runs proofs; never edits files. - **finding-sharing**: During parallel flux-drive reviews, agents can share high-severity findings via `{OUTPUT_DIR}/peer-findings.jsonl`. ([mistakeknot/interflux](https://github.com/mistakeknot/interflux/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-sharing.md) · ★1)
- **finding-skeptic**: Adversarial skeptic that tries to REFUTE a single recorded review finding against the unit diff; returns UPHELD / REFUTED-not-real / REFUTED-over-rated. Read-only. ([SyloRei/claude-godmode](https://github.com/SyloRei/claude-godmode/blob/HEAD/agents/finding-skeptic.md) · ★3)
- **finding-skeptic**: Use to try to refute one review finding before it is reported, one agent per finding per lens, dispatched by the review playbooks. - **finding-skeptic**: Adversarially verify hunter findings against source evidence, review scope, and catalog criteria. - **finding-skeptic**: Tries to refute one review finding and confirms it only with evidence. Use from /review for every finding of severity major or critical. ([slav-weber/agent-review-kit](https://github.com/slav-weber/agent-review-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/finding-skeptic.md))
- **finding-skeptic**: Adversarially verifies ONE code-review finding by trying to refute it against the actual code. Dispatched by the jira-tasks auto-review gate, one skeptic per finding. - **finding-synthesiser**: Synthesises the final finding for one subject by reconciling the say position (artefact derived) and the do position (interview derived) into a score, confidence, gap statement, and recommendations. - **finding-validator**: Adversarial validator for a candidate security finding. - **firebase-backend-security.reads-cache-offline.knowledge**: - Hydrating a page of parents with a per-parent SUBCOLLECTION (archive: BUT-1832 `message_query_module.dart`) has four failure modes: a per-item listener under `switchMap` over the parent stream rebuilds the WHOLE fan on every parent emission — key it by the parent-ID SET (`distinct`) so it only rebuilds on membership change; a `.take(n)` cap applied AFTER `.reversed` keeps the wrong end — cap on ([Malingisslen/butlery](https://github.com/Malingisslen/butlery/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/firebase-backend-security.reads-cache-offline.knowledge.md) · ★1)

- **firestore-rules-tester.probing.knowledge**: - Authenticated: `env.authenticatedContext(uid)` - Unauthenticated: `env.unauthenticatedContext()` - Guest (anonymous sign-in): `env.authenticatedContext(uid, { ...claims, firebase: { sign_in_provider: "anonymous", identities: {} } })`. `tokenOptions` is spread OVER a default whose `firebase.sign_in_provider` is `'custom'`, so the override replaces the whole `firebase` object; the emulator honours ([Malingisslen/butlery](https://github.com/Malingisslen/butlery/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/firestore-rules-tester.probing.knowledge.md) · ★1)
- **firestore-rules-tester.prose-claims.knowledge**: **A comment bounding that oracle by calling the id "unguessable"/"random" is a claim about EVERY MINTING PATH, and a collection usually has more than one** — group conversation ids are a Firestore auto-id from `createChatGroupWithDeps` AND a `sha256(ownerId:categoryId)[:20]` digest from `ensureCategoryChat`, so "group ids are random" is false for the second while the SAFETY conclusion still holds ([Malingisslen/butlery](https://github.com/Malingisslen/butlery/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/firestore-rules-tester.prose-claims.knowledge.md) · ★1)
- **firestore-rules-tester.server-written-and-removed.knowledge**: - **A collection with NO write limb still owes a CREATE-limb deny of its own.** The three verbs sent at a SEEDED document are an update and a delete — `set()` on an existing doc is an update — so the plausible future grant, `allow create: if isOwner(userId)` ("let the client initialise its own record"), is pinned by nothing. Measured on `user_moderation` (BUT-2046): the create mutant passes 18/18 ([Malingisslen/butlery](https://github.com/Malingisslen/butlery/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/firestore-rules-tester.server-written-and-removed.knowledge.md) · ★1)
- **firestore-rules-tester.vacuity.knowledge**: - **A nullable map has FOUR stored states, not two — absent, present-with-null, present-as-a-map-WITHOUT-the-key-you-chain-into, and fully populated — and each is a separate rule branch needing its own fixture.** The third is the one a short comment always omits and the one production writes most often (`Message.*Share` factories and `writeGroupSystemMessage` all store `metadata` as a map with no ([Malingisslen/butlery](https://github.com/Malingisslen/butlery/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/firestore-rules-tester.vacuity.knowledge.md) · ★1)

- **fiscal-auditoria-xml**: Audita XMLs de NF-e e NFC-e (separa CT-e e NFS-e) e entrega planilha de achados: CFOP × UF, CST/CSOSN × CRT, NCM, alíquotas, grupo IBS/CBS de 2026, chaves duplicadas, buracos de numeração e canceladas no faturamento. Use ao receber pasta de XMLs, faturamento por chave de acesso ou pedido de revisão das notas antes de apurar. Não use para apurar ICMS (fiscal-icms) nem gerar SPED (obrigacoes-efd-icms-ipi). ([adrianokelvio/agente-contabil](https://github.com/adrianokelvio/agente-contabil/blob/HEAD/agents/fiscal-auditoria-xml.md) · ★1)

Use ao implementar qualquer feature que toca tributo, nota fiscal, obrigação acessória, ou integração com SEFAZ/RFB. NÃO substitui consultor contábil humano — orienta o agente IA na arquitetura. ([roldaobatista/roldao-method](https://github.com/roldaobatista/roldao-method/blob/HEAD/templates/.claude/agents/fiscal-br.md))

Use ao receber XMLs de compra e venda, GNRE ou dúvida de ST e DIFAL. Não use para gerar o SPED Fiscal (obrigacoes-efd-icms-ipi). Also use proactively when the system encounters unknown fiscal scenarios or outdated information. - **fiscal-ipi**: Apura o IPI de indústria, importador e equiparado: enquadramento no RIPI, alíquota da TIPI pelo NCM, débitos, crédito de matéria-prima, produto intermediário e embalagem, suspensões, industrialização por encomenda e IPI na base do ICMS, com a transição de 2027. Use ao receber balancete ou razão de empresa do Real, ou pedido de estimativa, ajuste anual ou JCP.
